# ---- 2026-11-11: the themes and lists that were still Ukrainian (a list named again here adds to itself) ----

# from NpcMoreMindPatterns.cs (Mind)
[favour_no_examine] {не_огляд}

@не_огляд
I'm no trader to you.
Show it to someone who knows about such things.
My hands are dirty. I won't.
A thing is a thing. Why look at it?
~бурч I won't see anything in it. Off you go.
~весел Oh, I know nothing about these things! Spare me the shame.
~боязк What if I break it?

[favour_no_show] {не_покажу}

@не_покажу
My spot is mine. What do you want with it?
Some things you don't show.
Nothing interesting here. Move along.
~бурч Show you today, and tomorrow you'll be sitting there.
~стар Old folk need a corner nobody knows about, too.
~боязк There's… nothing there. Really.

[favour_no_sit] {не_посиджу}

@не_посиджу
I've got things to do. Well, not now, but soon.
I've no time for sitting.
Sit down yourself. The bench is free.
~бурч Sit with you? And I'd have to talk, too?
~весел I'd love to, but my feet keep carrying me off somewhere!
~смілив Sitting's not for me.

[favour_no_food] {не_їжа}

@не_їжа
It's not my storeroom to hand out.
We're on the edge ourselves. Sorry.
If the cook sees, it's my head.
~бурч And who'll give me some?
~весел I'd give you some, but I've already eaten it all!
~стар When food is plenty, everyone's generous. When it's scarce, some come begging.

[favour_no_gift] {не_подарунок}

@не_подарунок
This is all I have left.
I can't. I keep it for a reason.
You can remember without things.
~бурч Gifts are for feast days.
~весел Maybe someday! When I have two!
~стар I'll take it with me. Where I'm going.

[favour_no_secret] {не_таємниця}

@не_таємниця
Not now. Maybe someday.
That needs another evening.
Everyone has something they keep to themselves.
I'm not ready.
~бурч What secret? I'm an open book.
~весел What secret? I tell everyone everything!
~стар Some things are said only once. It's not time yet.

[favour_no_night] {не_вночі}

@не_вночі
At night? Out there? Go yourself.
At night I sleep. A fine habit, that.
I'm not that brave.
~бурч I don't want to go there by day, either.
~весел I'd go, but my blanket won't let me!
~стар Old bones don't walk at night.
~смілив Not tonight. I'm on watch in the morning.

# from NpcMoreStoryPatterns.cs (Story)
[mem_tale_gate] {с_чутка_брама}

[mem_tale_blue_fire] {с_чутка_вогонь}

[mem_tale_first_heart] {с_чутка_серце}

[idle ?npcremembers tale_gate] {вигук?} {с_чутка_брама}

[idle ?npcremembers tale_blue_fire] {вигук?} {с_чутка_вогонь}

[idle ?npcremembers tale_first_heart] {вигук?} {с_чутка_серце}

[night ?npcremembers tale_blue_fire] {с_ніч_вогонь}

[night ?npcremembers tale_gate] {с_ніч_брама}

[evening ?npcremembers tale_first_heart] {с_вечір_серце}

[see_player ?npcremembers tale_gate] {привіт} {с_питання_брама}

[see_player ?npcremembers tale_blue_fire] {привіт} {с_питання_вогонь}

[see_player ?npcremembers tale_first_heart] {привіт} {с_питання_серце}

[chat_open ?npcremembers tale_gate] {с_розмова_брама}

[chat_open ?npcremembers tale_blue_fire] {с_розмова_вогонь}

[chat_open ?npcremembers tale_first_heart] {с_розмова_серце}

@с_чутка_брама
those doors stayed shut longer than anyone here has lived. And now look.
makes you wonder what was in there, to be locked up like that.
I keep thinking about that gate. Who locks a house from the inside and then vanishes?
they say the hinges screamed across the whole valley. Stone doesn't scream for nothing.
if the doors have opened, sooner or later something will come out.
old halls aren't built for the living. That's what I think.
whoever built a gate like that was very afraid of something. Or someone.
I wouldn't go there alone for any coin. For a lot of coin, I'd think about it.
~бурч so they opened it. And who's going to shut it?
~весел what if there's treasure in there! Heaps! And nobody guarding it! No, I don't believe it.
~стар I saw that Hall from afar. It stands like a sleeping giant. Better not wake it.
~мол I want to see it! Just from the doorway. Well, maybe one step further.
~боязк why open something that was shut so well?
~смілив one day I'll look in there too. Whatever's inside.

@с_чутка_вогонь
a fire that gives no warmth. What's it for, then?
I look at our hearth and I'm glad it burns yellow.
only what shouldn't burn burns blue. My memory told me so, and don't ask how it knows.
a fire that gives no warmth likely has no mercy in it, either.
they say you can see that fire from far off. And they say not to stare at it too long.
I think not every fire should be lit. Even if you have the wood.
~бурч we're running out of firewood, and over there they burn a fire that doesn't even warm. Some masters.
~весел a blue fire! I'd like to see it. From far off. Very far off.
~стар I've seen many fires. Never one like that. And I don't want to.
~мол what if you held your hand to it? I'm not saying I would. Just asking.
~боязк I dream that our hearth turns blue, too.
~смілив a fire's a fire. Pour water on it, and we'll see what it has to say then.

@с_чутка_серце
someone decided to stay for real. So it can be done.
they say the lantern flared up and went out. Like someone waking for a moment.
I wonder if there's a place in the world where every heart of the home knows about the others.
if there's a heart, then there are those who won't be going anywhere anymore. Good or bad, who knows.
strange: you set a stone, and the place becomes yours.
~бурч so they set up a heart. Now there's a heart to feed, too.
~весел heart of the home! What a lovely name! Who thought of it?
~стар when I came here, nobody even thought of staying. The world is changing.
~мол can I have one too? A little one?
~боязк what if that strange fire in it brings something?
~смілив where there's a heart, there's something to defend. That's how it should be.

@с_ніч_вогонь
At night the horizon looks a little blue to me. It's just my eyes, right?
In the dark it's easy to believe in a blue fire. Easier in the morning.
~бурч Don't talk to me about that fire before bed.
~стар On nights like this the old go quiet, and the young, for some reason, ask questions.
~боязк Does anyone else see that light? Or just me?
~смілив If anything blue comes up to the wall, wake me.

@с_ніч_брама
I wonder if it stands open at night, too.
Doors creak differently at night. Ours too.
~боязк Did you hear that? Like a creak, far off. No?
~стар Someone opened it, but who'll stand at that door at night?

@с_вечір_серце
It's nice to think in the evening that somewhere such a heart already stands.
We sit down of an evening, and it's like we belong here. Like it's for good.
~стар I sit and wonder how many evenings that heart will have to hold out.
~весел Let's drink to whoever set the first one! Though I don't know who.

@с_питання_брама
Have you heard about the gate of the Hall?
They say someone opened it. You don't know who?
You weren't there, by any chance?
~бурч Don't tell me you were the one poking around in there.
~мол Were you there? What's inside?
~боязк Don't you go there, all right?

@с_питання_вогонь
Have you seen that blue fire?
Is it true it gives no warmth?
What do you make of that fire?
~бурч It wasn't you lighting something over there, was it?
~стар Careful with fires like that. I'm not joking.
~мол What's it like? Really blue?

@с_питання_серце
They say the first heart of the home is already up. Have you heard?
Who's the bold one who decided to settle first?
~весел Maybe you were the one who set the first heart?
~стар An important thing, that heart. Don't you joke with it.

@с_розмова_брама
They say the gate of the Hall isn't shut anymore.
Who went in there, do you think?
What could they have kept locked up so long?
Has anyone seen yet what's behind those doors?
~боязк Maybe it would be better if it had stayed shut.

@с_розмова_вогонь
A blue fire. Is that even possible?
They say that fire gives no warmth. What good is a fire like that?
Do you hear the talk about the Hall and the fire, too?
~стар There's been no news like this for a long time. And that was fine.

@с_розмова_серце
Somewhere the first heart of the home already stands.
So people are starting to stay. Is that good?
Do you think someone else will set up a heart like that?
~мол Maybe ours will be the biggest?

# from NpcMoreArcPatterns.cs (Arcs)
[promise_nag_request] {обіцяв_річ}

@обіцяв_річ
I'm in no hurry. Not yet.
You said you'd bring it. I'm still waiting.
I'm not rushing you. Just reminding you. You promised.
Remember what I asked for? You said: I'll bring it.
I've already made a place for it. Empty, for now.
How's it going with what you promised?
~бурч A promise costs nothing. Now I see why.
~весел I look out for you with it every day! Well, almost every day!
~стар I'll wait a while longer. I know how to wait. Not too long, though.
~боязк I don't want to be a bother. But you said…

@обіцяв_мрія
The way you said it back then, I even slept easy.
I never told anyone else about it. Only you.
You said you'd think about it. Have you?
I believed you then. That's what I wanted to say.
Remember what I told you about? You promised to think on it.
I'm not rushing you. But I remember it every evening.
~бурч I knew it. Everyone promises to think about it.
~весел So what did you come up with? I'm all ears!
~стар A dream can wait. Me, maybe not.
~мол You didn't forget? You didn't forget, right?

@обіцяв_поміч
I'm not complaining. I'm just reminding you that you promised.
You said: I'll think about it. You could finish thinking by now.
You said you'd do something about it. Nothing so far.
Nothing has changed since we talked.
Remember our talk? I do.
I'm still the same, if you care to know.
~бурч I said nobody would do anything. Said it to myself, mind you.
~весел Maybe today you'll remember it? I believe in you!
~стар Warm words, but it hasn't gotten any easier.
~боязк You haven't changed your mind, have you?

@обіцяв_їжа
You said there'd be food. I looked in the storeroom.
The storeroom's as empty as it was when you promised.
A belly can't hear promises. I'm still waiting.
I'm not asking for a feast. Just something, like you said.
You do remember the food, right? Because my stomach does.
When you said there'd be food, I believed you. I still do.
~бурч You can't fill up on promises. I've checked.
~весел I've already picked what I'll eat first! Now it just has to show up!
~стар At my age hunger is harder to bear. You did promise.
~боязк I don't want to be a bother. I'm just very hungry.

@обіцяв_платня
You said you'd pay. The treasury says otherwise.
I looked in the treasury. Same as it was back then.
I'm working. Remember what you promised me for it?
I remember the word about pay better than any coin.
You haven't forgotten the treasury, have you?
I'm not rushing you. But you said: I'll pay.
~бурч I know how to work for nothing. I haven't come to like it.
~весел I already know where every coin will go! Once there are any!
~стар I've seen plenty of promises. Coins, I've seen less often.
~боязк I'm not complaining. It's just that the treasury is still empty.

@обіцяв_слово
Maybe you just haven't met them yet? Let me help: there they are, {arc_other}.
Every morning I look at you and think: today. Not yet, though.
You promised to talk. {arc_other} still looks at me the same way.
Have you talked yet? Looks like you haven't.
I'm waiting. You said you'd talk.
A word was given. I remember it. {arc_other} will remember soon too, if you speak up.
~бурч Fine, never mind. I'll manage somehow on my own.
~боязк I'm scared, but tell them. Please.
~мол So when? When will you tell them?

@обіцянку_знято
Thank you for not just keeping quiet.
Well, at least now I know not to wait.
All right. Thanks for at least telling me.
It didn't work out, so it didn't. I hold no grudge.

Better this than silence.
~бурч Uh-huh. I thought as much.
~весел That's all right! I'm glad you tried anyway!
~стар I'm used to it by now. Don't worry about it.
~боязк It's all right, it's all right. I wasn't expecting anything.

@слово_дотримав
You said it, and I didn't believe you. Now I feel awkward.
Just like that? Said it and did it? Thank you.
You actually did it. I won't forget.
Now that's something. Said and done.

# Torvald's tall tales (assets/story/words/torvald.txt): the same parts, the same counts, in the same order
[torvald:tall_tale] {торвальд_вступ} {торвальд_зачин} {торвальд_поворот} {торвальд_кінець?}
[torvald:told_tale] {торвальд_почин} {торвальд_зачин} {торвальд_поворот} {торвальд_кінець?}
[torvald:told_tale] {торвальд_зачин} {торвальд_поворот} {торвальд_кінець?}

@торвальд_почин
Well, listen.
Oh, that I can do!
It was… long ago. Or yesterday.
Sit closer.
Aha, there's one!
Heh! So, listen.
Tell you? I'll tell you!
Oh, I know one, and a true one at that.

@торвальд_вступ
Wait-wait!
Oh! That reminds me.
Listen, listen, this is important!
Stop. Did I tell you?
By the way!
Oh, it's like that time!
Wait, listen!
Oh, I remember!
Hush!
Listen, an adventure!
You've reminded me of something.
Hold on, let me tell you.
Aha! Listen here.
That's just how it was with me!
Oh, don't interrupt, or I'll forget!
Quiet, quiet, here comes the good part.

@торвальд_зачин
I once quarrelled with a troll over a stump.
I had a boar once, a learned one: it counted to seven.
I went fishing once and sat so long I forgot who was catching whom.
I drank mead once from Odin's own horn, or maybe from the horn of the goat standing next to him.
I told the grey dwarfs a story once, so long they fell asleep standing.
I once tangled the key to my house in my beard.
I once outran a deer with lightning on its antlers: it had my hat.
A watchman and I were building a wall: he laid a stone, I laid a stone.
The bog thing, the one with duckweed on its mug, borrowed my spoon.
I sneezed once so hard that every raven flew off the roof.
I once told the wolves there was cheese on the moon.
I went for firewood once and came back for water.
I planted a turnip once, and an axe grew.
I once borrowed fog from the bog to hide from Heid.
I once taught a hen to fly.
I once sold my shadow to a dvergr, for three coppers.
I once played dice with a dead man from a barrow.
I once hid winter in a sack so I wouldn't freeze.
I once had a drinking race with a giant.
I once found a door in the forest, with no house to it.
I once kept bees that gathered not honey but gossip.
I once got lost in my own house.
I once pinched the wind's hat.
I once quarrelled with my own shadow over which of us is older.
I once wove a net out of moonlight.
I once herded clouds on the Meadows.
I once borrowed a raven's voice, for one song.
I once cooked soup from stones.
I once tried to catch lightning in a bucket.
I once courted a mermaid, but she turned out to live on dry land.
I once went after a bear with a spoon.
I once woke a dragon to ask the way.
I once counted the stars, so none would go missing.
I once borrowed a bear's den, for one winter.
I once washed a boar in the river, because Heid said it smelled.
I once caught fish with my hat.
I once taught a wolf to bark.
I once carved a spoon out of a deer's antler, and the deer was against it.
I once argued with the echo in a cave over who'd have the last word.
I once hid the sun under a bucket so I could sleep in.
I once carried an egg from a dragon's nest, to make pancakes.
I once sailed across the bog in a washtub.
I once traded a troll a stone for a stone.
I once went to a wake with the dead in a barrow: they invited me themselves.

@торвальд_поворот
We sat like that for three days, till the stump put down roots and walked away from us both.
At the eighth time it all went wrong and I got bitten. Eight's been an unlucky number for me ever since.
We agreed to take turns: now I eat fish on Tuesdays, and the fish never eats me, because I'm tough.
For three days I spoke only in verse. Four verses. I didn't know any more.
Ever since, when they see me, they run. Out of respect.
Found it a year later: by then there was a house there too, and the key fit.
Never got the hat back. Still worn, I've seen it. Suits it.
And the thing went and grew the other way. Still haven't found out whose fault it was.
Promised to give it back "as soon as it dries". Still waiting. That must be why the spoon's gone.
Only two came back. The rest, they say, are still flying. Give them my regards.
Too ashamed to own up now. You won't tell anyone, will you?
And I brought fire. Heid says that can't happen. But I brought it! Well, got a little burnt.
Chopped with it all winter after. Tasty, it was.
Never gave it back. That's why it's always damp around here.
The hen learned. I didn't. Now she looks down on me.
Now I go without one. Cool in summer, awkward in winter.
Lost all my teeth. My own. The dead man still won't give them back.
In spring the sack came undone. That's why we get snow in June.
The giant fell first. On me. That's how I got a bit flatter.
I opened it — and there was yesterday. I shut it quick.
Now I know everything about everyone in the camp. But I won't tell. Almost.
They looked for me for three days. Found me in a chest. I'd settled in by then.
Now the wind blows from one side only. It sulks.
Still haven't settled it. We go about the two of us, arguing.
Caught two stars and one bat. The bat was not pleased.
One got away. Now when it rains — that's her crying.
Paid it back with extra: now I caw in the mornings.
Turned out not bad, only my teeth still ache.
Caught half. The other half's still running about somewhere.
It didn't work out: she wanted to live in a well, and I by the stove.
We laughed so hard we fell off a tree. That's how I won.
They told me "left". I went right. Been lost ever since.
Now the thing follows me around and asks for more.
I came back a week later, and nobody knew me. Not even me.
